DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

DOES THE STATEMENT ADD UP
  [holds      ] total income = revenue from operations + other income
  [holds      ] profit before exceptional items = total income - total expenses
  [holds      ] total tax = current tax + deferred tax
  [holds      ] profit from continuing operations = profit before tax - tax
  [holds      ] total comprehensive income = profit + other comprehensive income

  Only relationships that hold by definition of a profit and loss account
  are tested. Decomposition - a total against the lines beneath it - is NOT
  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.

  checked: the three XBRL taxonomies in use name those lines differently and
  the rule failed on healthy filings, so it was removed rather than shipped.
